# W6 open world

World6 starts as World4's 18-slot ring. Every `birth_period` decisions, one of the 17 slots that are not Z dies and a new room is born there. The newborn's kind is uniform on the 7 H20 processes plus N. T_open is the mean of the checkpoint score at decisions L/8, 2L/8, …, L. The checkpoint score is bits/symbol of the frozen bank on a fresh EVAL=2048 continuation of each slot that holds a learnable room then, from a zero state, on that slot's channel row, from a copy of the world at that checkpoint. The final score is the checkpoint at L. Lower is better. The anchor is rand_dwell_s with birth_period None, the closed World4 life, scored with the eight checkpoints.

Every policy sleeps for the fixed share 0.20, in visits of 8, placed as in W5. At L = 4096 that is 816 decisions, share 0.199219. prog_age_s is M8's ageing bandit with tau 256, EPS 0.1, BETA 0.3, arms = slots. It is not told about births.

## Birth logs

One log per seed and period. The same log was checked against every policy, and against an independent draw from default_rng(world_seed + 90000): slot uniform on the 17 non-Z ring positions, then kind uniform on G, C1, C7, E30, E110, M7, M37, N. k starts at 0. A learnable newborn is h20_world.World(world_seed + 3000 + k). Kind N uses default_rng(world_seed + 95000 + k). birth_period None has no births. The channel column is the slot's initial room, which does not change.

## Decision shares

A stay counts for the slot. Noise is the share of decisions that were stays in a slot whose room was N at that decision, initial N1–N3 or a newborn N. A move counts for the corridor. A sleep decision counts for sleep. Slot shares, corridor and sleep sum to 1. Noise is part of the slot shares.

## Birth latency

Decisions from a birth until the next stay in that slot. If the life ends first, the latency is L minus the birth decision and the birth is censored. The median and the mean include censored latencies.

## Protocol

- World6(world_seed, birth_period) is World4(world_seed). birth_period None does not birth.
- After decision t, for every positive multiple t = birth_period, 2·birth_period, … that is at most L, one slot is drawn uniformly from the 17 that are not Z, in ring order, with default_rng(world_seed + 90000). The kind is then drawn uniformly from G, C1, C7, E30, E110, M7, M37, N. A learnable kind reads h20_world.World(world_seed + 3000 + k) from its beginning. Kind N is uniform on 41 symbols with default_rng(world_seed + 95000 + k). k counts births from 0. The slot keeps its ring position and its channel row, the initial room's row. Sleep and corridor decisions are on the same clock.
- A checkpoint at the same decision scores the world after that birth. The score is the mean over slots whose current room is learnable. EVAL=2048 symbols come from a copy of that room, from a zero state, on the slot's channel row. The live life then continues.
- The cortex, stores, coin, sleep dose and sleep placement are W5's, which are W4's. Policy rng is default_rng(1000 + world_seed). prog_age_s's choice rng is default_rng(10000 + world_seed), as in M8. Dwell is 8. A tie on the ring goes left.
- oracle_learn_s targets a learnable slot, uniform, at each choice. oracle_new_s keeps S, the learnable slots whose current room was born since it last arrived there. S starts as every learnable slot. The target is uniform over S, or over the learnable slots when S is empty. The first stay of the visit removes that slot from S. A later learnable birth puts it back.
- prog_age_s ranks slots by v + (v_max − v) · (1 − exp(−age / 256)) on the greedy step. The room reward is bits saved on the slot's exam glances over the visit, divided by the visit's decisions. Travel counts. Sleep is not a bandit update. The stored EMA is not aged.
- World seeds 0, 1, 2, 3, 4 and 5. L = 4096. birth_period 128 and 512 for every policy, plus rand_dwell_s with birth_period None. CPU. Private Kaggle kernels, pool of 4, no GPU, no internet. Nothing retuned.
